| rcef.canonical_expression.brow_inner_raise_left | Inner brow raise, left |  | left | 1 | 0.0 to 1.0 | mapped | The inner part of the left brow lifts, and the forehead above it lifts with it.<br>Calibration: rcef.canonical.v1:brow_inner_raise_left: declared 0 = neutral face, 1 = full activation of the action RCEF defines; assumed to agree with each source at 0 and 1; values between are passed through by the hop operator, and RCEF assumes, without evidence, that each source's in-between values correspond in this way; no measured gain or response curve<br>Neutral: 0.0<br>Evidence: rcef_canonical_v1, facs_wikipedia |

## rcef.canonical_viseme: RCEF canonical viseme set (draft)

| Signal | Name | Native index | Side | Units | Native range | Status | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| rcef.canonical_viseme.sil | Rest: no speech articulation |  | shared | probability | 0.0 to 1.0 | mapped | No speech articulation: silence and pauses. sil is not an IPA segment, and RCEF asserts no mouth pose for it.<br>Calibration: rcef.canonical.v1:sil: probability of the IPA shape class (docs/canonical-set.md) as the source reports it; no per-class neutral; rest is sil = 1<br>Evidence: rcef_canonical_v1, ipa_chart_doulos_2020 |
| rcef.canonical_viseme.bilabial | Bilabial: lips closed or close together |  | shared | probability | 0.0 to 1.0 | mapped | RCEF class: bilabial consonants; the lips close or come close together.<br>Calibration: rcef.canonical.v1:bilabial: probability of the IPA shape class (docs/canonical-set.md) as the source reports it; no per-class neutral; rest is sil = 1<br>Evidence: rcef_canonical_v1, ipa_chart_doulos_2020 |
| rcef.canonical_viseme.labiodental | Labiodental: lower lip to upper teeth |  | shared | probability | 0.0 to 1.0 | mapped | RCEF class: labiodental consonants; the lower lip touches the upper teeth.<br>Calibration: rcef.canonical.v1:labiodental: probability of the IPA shape class (docs/canonical-set.md) as the source reports it; no per-class neutral; rest is sil = 1<br>Evidence: rcef_canonical_v1, ipa_chart_doulos_2020 |
| rcef.canonical_viseme.dental | Dental fricative, interdental or linguolabial: tongue tip visible |  | shared | probability | 0.0 to 1.0 | mapped | RCEF class: dental fricatives, interdental and linguolabial coronals; the tongue tip shows at or between the teeth, or at the upper lip. IPA dental stops, nasals and laterals (t̪ d̪ n̪ l̪) stay in the coronal classes.<br>Calibration: rcef.canonical.v1:dental: probability of the IPA shape class (docs/canonical-set.md) as the source reports it; no per-class neutral; rest is sil = 1<br>Evidence: rcef_canonical_v1, ipa_chart_doulos_2020 |
